Ankalaev]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Strickland vs. Imavov]] \n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Cannonier vs. Strickland''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 216, UFC on ESPN+ 74''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 66''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on December 17, 2022, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-news-2022-schedule-ends-seven-events-las-vegas|title=NAC approves seven UFC event licenses for remainder of 2022, including six UFC Apex shows|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-09-20|access-date=2022-09-20}}\n\n==Background==\nA middleweight bout between former [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] challenger [[Jared Cannonier]] and [[Sean Strickland]] served as the main event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/09/ufc-news-sean-strickland-vs-jared-cannoiner-rebooked-dec-17-fight-night-main-event|title=Jared Cannonier vs. Sean Strickland to headline UFC Fight Night event on Dec. 17|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2022-09-13|access-date=2022-09-13}} The pairing was previously scheduled to headline [[UFC Fight Night: Grasso vs. Araújo]], but it was scrapped after Strickland withdrew due to a finger infection.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/8/30/23330014/sean-strickland-out-of-headliner-with-jared-cannonier-at-ufc-event-on-oct-15|title=Sean Strickland out of headliner with Jared Cannonier at UFC event on Oct. 15|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2022-08-30|access-date=2022-08-30}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Bruno Silva (fighter)|Bruno Silva]] and Albert Duraev was scheduled to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/9/1/23333070/bruno-silva-albert-duraev-added-ufc-final-card-2022|title=Bruno Silva vs. Albert Duraev added to UFC's final card of 2022|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2022-09-01|access-date=2022-09-01}} However, Silva withdrew due to an injury and was replaced by [[Michał Oleksiejczuk]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/9/20/23363028/michal-oleksiejczuk-replaces-bruno-silva-albert-duraev-ufc-2022|title=Michal Oleksiejczuk replaces Bruno Silva vs. Albert Duraev at UFC's final card of 2022|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2022-09-20|access-date=2022-09-20}} In turn, Duraev was forced to withdraw for undisclosed reasons and was replaced by [[Cody Brundage]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/12/ufc-fight-night-216-albert-duraev-out-cody-brundage-vs-michal-oleksiejczuk-mma-booking-announncement-matchup?taid=6389ee5dc7dd5a0001626c59|title=With Albert Duraev out, Cody Brundage steps in to face Michal Oleksiejczuk at UFC Fight Night 216|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun and Nolan King|date=2022-12-02|access-date=2022-12-02}}\n\nFormer [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Alex Perez (fighter)|Alex Perez]] was expected to face [[Amir Albazi]] at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2022/8/30/23329359/alex-perez-vs-amir-albazi-slated-for-ufc-fight-night-in-december-mma-news|title=Alex Perez vs. Amir Albazi slated for UFC Fight Night in December|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Mookie Alexander|date=2022-08-30|access-date=2022-08-31|archive-date=2022-08-31|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220831015244/https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2022/8/30/23329359/alex-perez-vs-amir-albazi-slated-for-ufc-fight-night-in-december-mma-news|url-status=dead}} However, he pulled out due to undisclosed reasons in late October and was replaced by [[Brandon Royval]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/10/ufc-news-brandon-royval-vs-amir-albazi-booked-dec-17|title=Brandon Royval vs. Amir Albazi booked for UFC Fight Night on Dec. 17|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2022-10-25|access-date=2022-10-25}} In turn, Royval withdrew from the card in late November due to a broken wrist during training and was replaced by former [[LUX Fight League#Men's Flyweight Championship|LUX Fight League]] Flyweight Champion Alessandro Costa.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/11/ufc-news-brandon-royval-out-vs-amir-albazi-fight-night-216-injury|title=Brandon Royval announces he's out of UFC Fight Night 216 due to broken wrist|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-11-23|access-date=2022-11-23}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.itnwwe.com/mma/alessandro-costa-vs-amir-albazi-at-ufc-vegas-66/|title=Alessandro Costa replaced Royval against Amir Albazi at UFC Vegas 66|publisher=itnwwe.com|author=Arshad|date=2022-11-23|access-date=2022-11-23}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Tafon Nchukwi]] and Jamal Pogues was expected to take the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Tafon-Nchukwi-vs-Jamal-Pogues-Booked-for-UFC-Fight-Night-on-Dec-17-187803|title=Tafon Nchukwi vs. Jamal Pogues booked for UFC Fight Night on Dec. 17|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2022-08-24|access-date=2022-08-25}} However, Pogues pull out from the bout due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by [[Vitor Petrino]].{{Cite web|url=https://ge.globo.com/combate/noticia/2022/11/03/ufc-vitor-petrino-tem-estreia-marcada-contra-tafon-nchukwi.ghtml|title=UFC: Vitor Petrino has his debut scheduled against Tafon Nchukwi|publisher=ge.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2022-11-03|access-date=2022-11-03}} {{in lang|pt-br}} Nchukwi eventually pulled out of the bout himself due to undisclosed reasons and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web |last=Sarmah |first=Manjit |title=Breaking: UFC Fight Night: Cannonier vs. Strickland fighter Tafon Nchukwi pulls out due to injury |url=https://www.sportskeeda.com/mma/news-breaking-ufc-fight-night-cannonier-vs-strickland-fighter-tafon-nchukwi-pulls-due-injury |access-date=2022-12-17 |website=www.sportskeeda.com |language=en-us}}\n\n[[Michael Morales (fighter)|Michael Morales]] was expected to face [[Rinat Fakhretdinov]] in a welterweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-bellator-mma-news-matchup-roundup-september-26-october-2|title=Matchup Roundup: New UFC and Bellator fights announced in the past week (Sept. 26-Oct. web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/11/ufc-fight-night-216-michael-morales-injured-out-vs-rinat-fakhretdinov|title=Unbeaten Michael Morales injured, out of UFC Fight Night 216 vs. Rinat middleweight winner Bryan Battle.{{Cite web|url=https://www.itnwwe.com/mma/ufc-vegas-66-bryan-battle-vs-rinat-fakhretdinov/|title=UFC Vegas 66: Bryan Battle Replaced Morales Against Rinat Fakhretdinov|publisher=itswwe.com|author=Arshad|date=2022-12-02|access-date=2022-12-02}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Julian Marquez]] and [[Deron Winn]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/08/ufc-news-julian-marquez-vs-deron-winn-fight|title=UFC books Julian Marquez vs. Deron Winn for Dec. 17|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-08-27|access-date=2022-12-15}} However, just two days before the event, Winn was forced to withdraw after fainting and falling down a set of stairs, sustaining a minor [[concussion]]. As a result, the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/12/15/23511598/deron-winn-forced-out-ufc-vegas-66-after-fainting-falling-down-stairs-ufc-performance-institute|title=Deron Winn out of UFC Vegas 66 after fainting, falling down stairs at UFC Performance Institute|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2022-12-15|access-date=2022-12-15}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Hayisaer Maheshate weighed in at 158.5 pounds, two and a half pounds over the lightweight non-title fight limit. His bout proceeded at catchweight and he was fined 20% of his purse, which went to his opponent [[Rafa García (fighter)|Rafa García]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/12/16/23513032/ufc-vegas-66-weigh-in-results-maheshate-misses-weight-by-2-5-pounds|title=UFC Vegas 66 weigh-in results: Maheshate misses weight by 2.5 pounds|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Night: Grasso vs. Araújo]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Kattar vs. Allen]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 280: Oliveira vs. Makhachev''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on October 22, 2022, at the [[Etihad Arena]] in [[Abu Dhabi]], United Arab Emirates.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/3/18/22984624/blockbuster-ufc-281-october-abu-dhabi-crowd-pleasing-title-bout|title='Blockbuster' UFC 281 set for Oct. 22 in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2022-03-18|access-date=2022-03-18}}\n\n==Background==\nA [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] bout for the vacant title between former champion [[Charles Oliveira]] and [[Islam Makhachev]] headlined the event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/07/ufc-280-charles-oliveira-vs-islam-makhachev-vacant-title-fight-booked|title=Charles Oliveira vs. Islam Makhachev vacant title fight set for UFC 280 in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2022-07-16|access-date=2022-07-16}} Oliveira was stripped of the title at [[UFC 274]] when he failed to make weight for his attempted title defense against former interim champion (also former [[WSOF Lightweight Championship|WSOF Lightweight Champion]]) [[Justin Gaethje]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/05/ufc-274-weigh-in-results-and-live-video-stream-noon-et|title=UFC 274 weigh-in results: Charles Oliveira misses weight, stripped of title|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2022-05-06|access-date=2022-07-16}} [[Beneil Dariush]], who is scheduled to face former [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Featherweight championship|KSW Featherweight]] and [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Lightweight championship|Lightweight Champion]] [[Mateusz Gamrot]], revealed in mid-September that he was expected to serve as backup to this contest.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/9/15/23355057/beneil-dariush-reveals-expected-to-serve-as-backup-to-charles-oliveira-vs-islam-makhachev-at-ufc-280|title=Beneil Dariush reveals he's expected to serve as backup to Charles Oliveira vs. Islam Makhachev at UFC 280|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2022-09-15|access-date=2022-09-15}} In turn, current [[UFC Featherweight Championship|UFC Featherweight Champion]] [[Alexander Volkanovski]] announced in mid-October that he was the official replacement in case either of the headliners pull out.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/10/ufc-280-alexander-volkanovski-backup-charles-oliveira-vs-islam-makhachev|title=Alexander Volkanovski says he's UFC 280 title-fight backup for Charles Oliveira vs. Islam Makhachev|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2022-10-10|access-date=2022-10-10}} Dariush still claimed that he had the backup spot, but no official announcement had been made by the promotion.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/10/ufc-280-news-beneil-dariush-confused-by-alexander-volkanovski-title-fight-backup-claim|title=Beneil Dariush confused by Volkanovski's UFC 280 backup claims|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2022-10-11|access-date=2022-10-11}} Volkanovski eventually weighed in successfully as the official substitute.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/10/21/23415904/ufc-280-weigh-in-results-charles-oliveira-aljamain-sterling-championship-katlyn-chookagian-misses|title=UFC 280 weigh-in results: Charles Oliveira, Aljamain Sterling on point for championship bouts, Katlyn Chookagian misses|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2022-10-21|access-date=2022-10-21}}\n\nA [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Aljamain Sterling]] and former two-time champion [[T.J. Dillashaw]] was originally targeted as the main event of [[UFC 279]], but the promotion opted to change plans and move it to this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/07/ufc-279-aljamain-sterling-vs-tj-dillashaw-nate-diaz-khamzat-chimaev|title=Aljamain Sterling vs. T.J. Dillashaw no longer on UFC 279 table, but possible for UFC 280|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-07-19|access-date=2022-07-19}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/07/ufc-280-news-aljamain-sterling-vs-tj-dillashaw-title-fight-rescheduled-abu-dhabi|title=Aljamain Sterling vs. T.J. Dillashaw title fight rescheduled for UFC 280 in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2022-07-20|access-date=2022-07-20}}\n\nFormer [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Katlyn Chookagian]] and [[Manon Fiorot]] met in a women's flyweight bout at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/7/26/23279495/katlyn-chookagian-vs-manon-fiorot-shifts-to-ufc-280|title=Katlyn Chookagian vs. Manon Fiorot shifts to UFC 280|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2022-07-26|access-date=2022-07-26}} They were originally scheduled to meet at [[UFC Fight Night: Gane vs. Tuivasa]] a month earlier, with Chookagian pulling out once due to undisclosed reasons, only to return as a replacement for her own substitute.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Katlyn-Chookagian-vs-Manon-Fiorot-Womens-Flyweight-Bout-Added-to-UFC-Paris-186901|title=Katlyn Chookagian vs. Manon Fiorot women's flyweight bout added to UFC Paris|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tudor Leonte|date=2022-05-26|access-date=2022-06-15}}{{Cite web|url=https://empiresportsmedia.com/mma/katlyn-chookagian-out-jessica-andrade-in-to-face-manon-fiorot-at-ufc-paris|title=Katlyn Chookagian out, Jessica Andrade in to face Manon Fiorot at UFC Paris|publisher=empiresportsmedia.com|author=Daniel Cunningham|date=2022-06-15|access-date=2022-06-15}}{{Cite web|url=https://rmcsport.bfmtv.com/sports-de-combat/mma/ufc-paris-andrade-forfait-ca-devrait-etre-chookagian-pour-manon-fiorot_AV-202207160323.html|title=UFC Paris: Andrade withdrew, it should be Chookagian for Manon Fiorot|publisher=rmcsport.bfmtv.com|author=Alexandre Herbinet|date=2022-07-16|access-date=2022-07-17}} {{in lang|fr}} At the weigh-ins, Chookagian weighed in at 127.5 pounds, one and a half pounds over the flyweight non-title fight limit. The bout proceeded at catchweight and she was fined 20% of her purse, which went to Fiorot.\n\nA women's strawweight bout between [[Marina Rodriguez]] and [[Amanda Lemos]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/07/ufc-280-amanda-lemos-vs-marina-rodriguez-booked|title=UFC 280 continues to stack lineup with Amanda Lemos vs. Marina Rodriguez targeted|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-07-21|access-date=2022-09-04}} However, the bout was postponed to [[UFC Fight Night 214]] for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/9/2/23334746/marina-rodriguez-vs-amanda-lemos-moved-from-ufc-280-to-nov-5-event|title=Marina Rodriguez vs. Amanda Lemos moved from UFC 280 to Nov. 5 event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2022-09-02|access-date=2022-09-04}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Parker Porter]] and [[Hamdy Abdelwahab]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/08/ufc-280-adds-hamdy-abdelwahab-vs-parker-porter-heavyweight-bout|title=UFC 280 adds Hamdy Abdelwahab vs. Parker Porter heavyweight bout|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Farah Hannoun|date=2022-08-17|access-date=2022-07-17}} However, Abdelwahab was removed for unknown reasons and replaced by promotional newcomer Slim Trabelsi.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Parker-Porter-to-Face-Newcomer-Slim-Trabelsi-at-UFC-280-in-Abu-Dhabi-187949|title=Parker Porter to face newcomer Slim Trabelsi at UFC 280 in Abu Dhabi|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2022-09-12|access-date=2022-09-13}} In turn, the pairing was cancelled altogether as Trabelsi pulled out due to contractual issues with ARES FC and Porter opted to pursue a later fight, rather than a replacement barring unforeseen circumstances.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-280-news-slim-trablesi-parker-porter-jamie-mullarkey-off|title=Three fighters off UFC 280 card in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Farah Hannoun|date=2022-09-18|access-date=2022-09-18}}\n\n[[Jamie Mullarkey]] was expected to face [[Magomed Mustafaev]] in a lightweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://wmmaa.org/magomed-mustafaev-to-perform-at-ufc-280-in-abu-dhabi/|title=Magomed Mustafaev to perform at UFC 280 in Abu Dhabi|publisher=wmmaa.org|author=Staff|date=2022-08-25|access-date=2022-08-26}} However, Mullarkey pulled out in mid-September due to injury. He was replaced by Yamato Nishikawa.{{Cite web|url=https://wmmaa.org/magomed-mustafaev-appointed-a-new-opponent/|title=Magomed Mustafaev appointed a new opponent|publisher=wmma.org|author=Staff|date=2022-09-24|access-date=2022-09-24}} In turn, Nishikawa was forced to withdraw due to contractual issues and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite [[UFC 281|UFC 281: Adesanya vs. Pereira]] \n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Rodriguez vs. Lemos''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 214, UFC on ESPN+ 72''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 64''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on November 5, 2022 at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-news-2022-schedule-ends-seven-events-las-vegas|title=NAC approves seven UFC event licenses for remainder of 2022, including six UFC Apex shows|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-09-20|access-date=2022-09-20}}\n\n==Background==\nA featherweight bout between [[Bryce Mitchell]] and [[Movsar Evloev]] was expected to headline the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/34692275/bryce-mitchell-movsar-evloev-featherweight-bout-set-ufc-fight-night-card-nov-5|title=Bryce Mitchell-Movsar Evloev featherweight bout set for UFC Fight Night card on Nov. 5|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2022-09-29|access-date=2022-09-29}} However, Evloev withdrew in mid October due to injury and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/10/13/23403895/movsar-evloev-out-of-upcoming-main-event-fight-against-bryce-mitchell-on-nov-5|title=Movsar Evloev out of upcoming main event fight against Bryce Mitchell on Nov. 5|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2022-10-13|access-date=2022-10-13}}\n\nA women's strawweight bout between [[Marina Rodriguez]] and [[Amanda Lemos]] was originally expected to take place at [[UFC 280]], but was postponed to this event for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/9/2/23334746/marina-rodriguez-vs-amanda-lemos-moved-from-ufc-280-to-nov-5-event|title=Marina Rodriguez vs. Amanda Lemos moved from UFC 280 to Nov. 5 event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2022-09-02|access-date=2022-09-04}} After the original main event was cancelled, they were promoted to the headliner spot.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/10/ufc-fight-night-214-marina-rodriguez-vs-amanda-lemos-new-main-event-bryce-mitchell-out|title=Bryce Mitchell has to wait: Marina Rodriguez vs. Amanda Lemos new UFC Fight Night 214 headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2022-10-23|access-date=2022-10-23}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[Miranda Maverick]] and [[Shanna scheduled for [[UFC 278]], but the bout was cancelled on the day of the weigh-ins as Young was hospitalized due to weight cut issues.{{cite Almeida was in need of a new opponent as [[Shamil Abdurakhimov]] pulled out due to undisclosed reasons of their heavyweight bout at [[UFC 280]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-bellator-mma-news-matchup-roundup-october-3-9|title=Matchup Roundup: New UFC and Bellator fights announced in the past week (Oct. pounds, one and a half pounds over the lightweight non-title fight limit.\n*[[Benito Lopez]] weighed in at 138.5 pounds, two and a half pounds over the bantamweight non-title fight limit.\n*Carlos Candelario weighed in at 127.5 pounds, one and a half pounds over the flyweight non-title fight limit.\n*Ramona Pascual weighed in at 137 pounds, one pound over the bantamweight non-title limit. \n\nAll four bouts proceeded at catchweight. Dawson was fined 30% of his purse while Lopez, Candelario, and Pascual were fined 20% of their individual purses which went to their opponents [[Mark Madsen (fighter)|Mark Madsen]], [[Mario Bautista]], Jake Hadley, and Tamires Vidal, respectively.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/11/ufc-fight-night-214-marina-rodriguez-amanda-lemos-weigh-in-results|title=UFC Fight Night Bautista''', '''Polyana Viana''', and '''Tamires Vidal'''\n\n== James Krause betting controversy ==\nJust before the event took place, the UFC was informed by sources that suspicious betting patterns had been observed for the fight between [[Darrick Minner]] and [[Shayilan Nuerdanbieke]]. Hours before the fight, Nuerdanbieke's odds to win moved significantly in his favor, thus causing the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]] to open an investigation.{{cite web | url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/11/ufc-review-suspicious-betting-activity-shayilan-nuerdanbieke-vs-darrick-minner | title=UFC to review suspicious betting activity before Shayilan Nuerdanbieke's win over Darrick Minner | date=7 November 2022 }} Suspicion arose when Minner, coached by UFC veteran [[James Krause (fighter)|James Krause]], threw a kick and showed signs of injury to his leg, kicking with that same leg once more before being finished by technical knockout seconds later. This caused many to speculate that Minner knowingly fought with an injury and purposely lost the fight, and that Krause, who had been very open about betting on fights, placed a bet on Nuerdanbieke. On November 18, the Nevada State Athletic Commission informed the UFC and Krause that Krause’s license was suspended and would remain so during the course of the investigation. The UFC also announced that any fighter who chose to continue to be coached by Krause or who continued to train in his gym would be banned from participating in future UFC events until the investigation concludes.{{cite web | url=https://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-statement-regarding-james-krause | title=UFC Statement Regarding James Krause | UFC | date=2 December 2022 }}\n\n== See also This record was surpassed on September 6, 2025, when [[UFC Fight Night: Imavov vs. Borralho]] recorded nine knockouts.{{Cite $11,562,807.77\n|previousevent = [[UFC Fight Night: Rodriguez vs. Lemos]]\n|followingevent = [[UFC Fight Night: Nzechukwu vs. Cuțelaba]]\n}} \n'''''UFC 281: Adesanya vs. Pereira''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on November 12, 2022, at the [[Madison Square Garden]] in [[New York City, New York]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2022/7/13/23207412/report-ufc-281-booked-nov-12-msg-new-york-city-mma|title=Report - UFC 281 set for Madison Square Garden landing pad on November 12.|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Jesse Holland|date=2022-07-13|access-date=2022-07-17}}\n\n==Background==\nA [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Israel Adesanya]] and former [[Glory (kickboxing)#Glory Middleweight Championship|Glory Middleweight]] and [[Glory (kickboxing)#Glory Light Heavyweight Championship|Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Alex Pereira]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/08/ufc-281-news-israel-adesanya-vs-alex-pereira-new-york-2022-madison-square-garden|title=UFC 281 set with Israel Adesanya vs. Alex Pereira headliner at Madison Square Garden|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-08-05|access-date=2022-08-05}} The pairing previously met twice in kickboxing matchups. The first in April 2016 which Pereira won by unanimous decision. Their second meeting took place at [[2017 in Glory of Heroes|Glory of Heroes 7]] in March 2017, which Pereira won by third-round knockout.{{Cite web|url=https://mmauk.net/2020/11/25/alex-pereira-and-israel-adesanya-an-undeniable-trilogy/|title=Alex Pereira and Israel Adesanya: an undeniable trilogy?|publisher=mmauk.net|author=Michael Owens|date=2020-11-25|access-date=2022-08-05}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] bout between current two-time champion [[Carla Esparza]] and former champion [[Zhang Weili]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/08/carla-esparza-announces-ufc-281-title-fight-vs-zhang-weili|title=Carla Esparza announces UFC 281 title fight vs. Zhang Weili|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-08-18|access-date=2022-08-18}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Matt Frevola]] and returning veteran [[Ottman Azaitar]] was rescheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/08/ufc-281-ottman-azaitar-vs-matt-frevola-booked-new-york|title=Ottman Azaitar vs. Matt Frevola rebooked for UFC 281 – after 'bag of potatoes' incident canceled first fight|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-08-22|access-date=2022-08-22}} The pairing was previously scheduled to meet in January 2021 at [[UFC 257]], but Azaitar was pulled from the bout and had his contract briefly terminated after it was determined that he had violated [[COVID-19]] health and safety protocols, as he attempted to help others enter the UFC's designated safety zone during fight week.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/1/22/22244418/ottman-azaitar-cut-from-ufc-after-violating-covid-19-safety-protocol|title=Ottman Azaitar cut from UFC after violating COVID-19 safety protocol|publisher=mmafighting.usatoday.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2021-01-22|access-date=2022-08-22}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Ryan Spann]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Undefeated]] lightweight winner [[Michael Trizano]] missed weight. Spann weighed in at 206.6 pounds, six tenths of a pound over the light heavyweight non-title fight limit. Trizano weighed in at 147.6 pounds, one and six tenths of a pound over the featherweight non-title fight limit. Both bouts proceeded at catchweight with each being fined 20% of their individual purses, which went to their opponents former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Dominick Reyes]] and [[Seung Woo Choi]] respectively.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/11/11/23453100/ufc-281-weigh-in-results-alex-pereira-makes-weight-at-last-minute-2-miss|title=UFC 281 weigh-in results: Alex Pereira makes weight at last minute, 2 miss|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2022-11-11|access-date=2022-11-11}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card}}\n{{MMAevent bout \n|Middleweight\n|[[Alex Pereira]]\n|def.\n|[[Israel Adesanya]] (c)\n|TKO Alex Pereira''' and '''Zhang Weili'''\n\n==Aftermath==\nWith eleven finishes, the event tied [[UFC Fight Night: Rockhold vs. Bisping]], [[UFC 224]], [[UFC Fight Night: Imavov vs. Borralho]], and [[UFC 329]] for [[List of UFC records#Most finishes – In a single event|most finishes in a single UFC event]]. The record was ultimately surpassed in August 2026 when [[UFC Fight Night: Medić vs. Rodriguez]] recorded twelve finishes.{{Cite web|url=https://heavy.com/sports/ufc/ufc-belgrade-bonuses-revealed-after-card/|title=UFC Belgrade bonuses revealed after record-setting card|publisher=heavy.com|author=Adam Martin|date=2026-08-01|access-date=2026-08-02}}\n\nThis event was also given the 2022 [[2022 in UFC#2022 UFC Honors awards|UFC Honors ''Event of the Year'']] award as voted by the fans.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/news/dana-whites-ufc-2022-presidents-choice-award-winners-revealed|title=Dana White's UFC 2022 President's Choice Award Winners Revealed|publisher=mmanews.com|author=J. Cunningham|date=2023-01-16|access-date=2024-01-13}}\n\n== See also ==\n{{Portal|Martial arts}}\n\n* [[List of UFC events]]\n* [[List of current UFC fighters]]\n* [[2022 in UFC]]\n\n== References ==\n{{Reflist}}\n\n{{UFC Events}}\n{{2022 in mixed martial arts}}\n\n[[Category:Ultimate Fighting Championship flagship events]]\n[[Category:2022 in mixed martial arts]]\n[[Category:2020s in Manhattan]]\n[[Category:2022 in sports in New York City]]\n[[Category:November 2022 sports events in the United States]]\n[[Category:Mixed martial arts events at Madison Square Garden]]\n[[Category:Ultimate Fighting Championship events in New York City]]"}}}],"langlinks":[{"lang":"ja","title":"UFC 281"}]},{"pageid":71312426,"ns":0,"title":"UFC 282","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{short description|Mixed martial arts event in 2022}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name= UFC 282: Błachowicz vs. Ankalaev\n|image= UFC_282_poster.jpg\n|promotion= [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date= {{start date|2022|12|10}}\n|venue= [[T-Mobile Arena]]\n|city= [[Paradise, Nevada]], United States\n|attendance= 18,455{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-282-post-event-facts-raul-rosas-jr-makes-history-youngest-winner-record-books|title=UFC 282 post-event facts: 18-year-old Raul Rosas Jr. etches name in record books with debut win|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2022-12-11|access-date=2022-12-11}}\n|gate= $4,409,511\n|previousevent= [[UFC on ESPN: Thompson vs. Holland]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Cannonier vs. Strickland]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 282: Błachowicz vs. Ankalaev''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]. It took place on December 10, 2022, at the [[T-Mobile Arena]] in [[Paradise, Nevada|Paradise]], [[Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-news-2022-schedule-ends-seven-events-las-vegas|title=NAC approves seven UFC event licenses for remainder of 2022, including six UFC Apex shows|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-09-20|access-date=2022-09-20}}\n\n==Background==\nA [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] rematch between then current champion [[Jiří Procházka]] (also former [[Rizin Fighting Federation#Light Heavyweight Championship|Rizin Light Heavyweight Champion]]) and former champion [[Glover Teixeira]] was originally expected to headline the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/10/ufc-282-jiri-prochazka-vs-glover-teixeira-rematch-booked-december-10|title=Jiri Prochazka vs. Glover Teixeira 2 booked for UFC 282 in December|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Samano|date=2022-10-13|access-date=2022-10-13}} The pair previously met at [[UFC 275]] in June, with Procházka winning the back and forth fight (and championship) by submission in the fifth round.{{Cite web|author=Jay Anderson|date=2022-06-12|title=UFC 275: Jiri Prochazka secures late submission, dethrones Glover Teixeira|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/06/12/ufc-275-glover-teixeira-vs-jiri-prochazka/|access-date=2022-08-12|publisher=cagesidepress.com}} However, on November 23, Procházka pulled out due to a shoulder injury that would require surgery and at least six months of rehabilitation. He decided to vacate the title, and Teixeira subsequently refused a replacement fight against [[Magomed Ankalaev]]. Therefore, the co-main event bout between former champion [[Jan Błachowicz]] (also former [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Light heavyweight championship|KSW Light Heavyweight Champion]]) and Ankalaev was promoted to the main event status and arranged for the vacant championship.{{Cite web|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/ufc-282-jiri-prochazka-injured-vacates-light-heavyweight-championship-blachowicz-ankalaev-title-bout-booked-212050487.html|title=UFC 282: Jiri Prochazka injured, vacates light heavyweight championship; Blachowicz-Ankalaev title bout booked|publisher=sports.yahoo.com|author=Kevin Iole|date=2022-11-23|access-date=2022-11-23}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/11/ufc-282-jiri-prochazka-statement-injury-shoulder|title=Jiri Prochazka apologizes for UFC 282 withdrawal, explains vacating light heavyweight title|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-11-23|access-date=2022-11-23}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/11/ufc-282-glover-teixeira-explains-turning-down-magomed-ankalaev-title-fight|title=Glover Teixeira on turning down makeshift UFC 282 title shot: I won't 'just show up to fight a guy because they say so'|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2022-11-24|access-date=2022-11-25}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Jairzinho Rozenstruik]] and [[Chris Daukaus]] was originally scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Dern vs. Yan]], but it was moved to this event for undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/09/ufc-282-news-chris-daukaus-vs-jairzinho-rozenstruik|title=UFC 282 lands Chris Daukaus vs. Jairzinho Rozenstruik after bout pushed back|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-09-18|access-date=2022-09-18}}\n\nPromotional newcomer [[Bo Nickal]] was expected to face [[Jamie Pickett]] in a middleweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/09/ufc-282-news-bo-nickal-debut-vs-jamie-pickett|title=Bo Nickal set to debut at UFC 282 vs. Jamie Pickett|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Mike Bohn|date=2022-09-29|access-date=2022-09-29}} However, it was announced in late October that Nickal withdrew due to injury and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/10/ufc-news-bo-nickal-debut-vs-jamie-pickett-off-delayed|title=Bo Nickal off UFC 282 vs. Jamie Pickett; promotion targeting new debut plans|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-10-21|access-date=2022-10-21}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between former title challenger [[Alexander Gustafsson]] and former interim title contender [[Ovince Saint Preux]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/10/11/23398581/alexander-gustafsson-to-fight-ovince-saint-preux-at-ufc-282|title=Alexander Gustafsson to fight Ovince Saint Preux at UFC 282|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2022-10-11|access-date=2022-10-11}} However, Gustafsson withdrew due to an undisclosed reason and was replaced by [[2018 in Professional Fighters League#2018 PFL Heavyweight playoffs|2018 PFL heavyweight tournament winner]] [[Philipe Lins]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkieu.usatoday.com/2022/11/ufc-282-news-ovince-saint-preux-alexander-gustafsson-philipe-lins|title=Ovince Saint Preux says Alexander Gustafsson out of UFC 282, Philipe Lins to replace|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-11-15|access-date=2022-11-15}} In turn, Lins withdrew from the bout due to an undisclosed reason and was replaced by Antonio Trocoli.{{Cite web|url=https://mma.uno/lins-fuera-antonio-trocoli-enfrenta-a-ovince-st-preux-en-ufc-282/|title=Lins out, Antonio Trocoli faces Ovince St. Preux at UFC 282|publisher=mma.uno|author=Steve Duncan|date=2022-11-24|access-date=2022-11-24}} {{in lang|es}} However, Trocoli withdrew due to visa issues.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/12/ufc-282-ovince-saint-preux-loses-third-opponent-antonio-trocoli-withdraws|title=Ovince Saint Preux loses third UFC 282 opponent after Antonio Trocoli withdraws|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2022-12-05|access-date=2022-12-05}} As a result, Saint Preux was pulled from the card as well due to the promotion being unable to find another replacement.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Ovince-St-Preux-Removed-from-UFC-282-After-Losing-Third-Potential-Opponent-188629/amp|title=Ovince St. Preux Removed from UFC 282 take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/platform/amp/2022/10/3/23385640/ronnie-lawrence-vs-cameron-saaiman-set-for-ufc-282|title=Ronnie Lawrence vs. Cameron Saaiman set for web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/8/16/23308860/robbie-lawler-set-to-face-santiago-ponzinibbio-at-ufc-282|title=Robbie Lawler set to face Santiago Ponzinibbio at UFC 282|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2022-08-16|access-date=2022-12-05}} The pairing was previously scheduled to meet at [[UFC 245]] in 2019 but Ponzinibbio withdrew due to a [[staph infection]].{{cite web|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2019/10/12/20910907/santiago-ponzinibbio-out-of-ufc-245-fight-with-robbie-lawler-welterweight-mma-news|title=Santiago Ponzinibbio out of UFC 245 However, the week of the event, the bout fell through again as Lawler was forced to withdraw due to an undisclosed injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/12/5/23494858/injured-robbie-lawler-out-of-ufc-282-fight-with-santiago-ponzinibbio|title=Injured Robbie Lawler out of UFC 282 fight with Santiago Ponzinibbio|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. place at the event.{{Cite Teixeira vs. Hill\n|promotion= [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|image= File:UFC 283 official poster.jpg\n|date= {{start date|2023|01|21}}\n|venue= [[Jeunesse Arena]]\n|city= [[Rio de Janeiro]], Brazil\n|attendance= 13,604{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/01/22/ufc-283-bonuses-main-event|title=UFC 283 main event earns Fight of the Night, Ismael Bonfim gets performance Lewis vs. Spivac]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 283: Teixeira vs. Hill''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on January 21, 2023, at the [[Jeunesse Arena]] in [[Rio de Janeiro]], [[Brazil]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/08/ufc-283-rio-de-janeiro-january-new-tv-deal-in-brazil|title=UFC announces return to Rio de Janeiro in January, new TV deal in Brazil|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2022-08-10|access-date=2022-08-10}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's return to Brazil for the first time since [[UFC Fight Night: Lee vs. Oliveira]], which took place in [[Brasília]] in March 2020.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/08/10/ufc-283-set-rio-de-janeiro-january-2023/|title=UFC 283 set for Rio de Janeiro in January 2023, promotion announces new brazilian broadcast plan|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2022-08-10|access-date=2022-08-26}} That event took place [[Behind closed doors (sport)|behind closed doors]] (for the first time in the promotion's history) after [[Ibaneis Rocha]], the [[Governor#Brazil|governor]] of the [[Federal District (Brazil)|Federal District]] (where Brasília is located), announced that large gatherings had been suspended due to the [[COVID-19 pandemic]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/ufc-on-espn-plus-28-brasilia-brazil-no-fans-coronavirus-pandemic-mma-sports|title=Coronavirus pushes UFC on ESPN+ 28 behind closed doors in Brasilia: Reports|author=Simon Head|date=2020-03-11|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|access-date=2020-03-12}}\n\nA [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] bout for the vacant title between former champion [[Glover Teixeira]] and [[Jamahal Hill]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/12/11/23503756/dana-white-glover-teixeira-vs-jamahal-hill-will-fight-for-vacant-title-at-ufc-283|title=Dana White: Glover Teixeira vs. Jamahal Hill will fight for vacant title at UFC 283|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2022-12-11|access-date=2022-12-11}} Teixeira was originally expected to compete at [[UFC 282]] as a challenger in a rematch against then-champion [[Jiří Procházka]], but Procházka pulled out and vacated the title after a serious shoulder injury. Teixeira subsequently refused a replacement fight against [[Magomed Ankalaev]], which prompted the UFC to change the original co-main event between former champion [[Jan Błachowicz]] and Ankalaev into the new title fight for the vacant belt.{{Cite web|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/ufc-282-jiri-prochazka-injured-vacates-light-heavyweight-championship-blachowicz-ankalaev-title-bout-booked-212050487.html|title=UFC 282: Jiri Prochazka injured, vacates light heavyweight championship; Blachowicz-Ankalaev title bout booked|publisher=sports.yahoo.com|author=Kevin Iole|date=2022-11-23|access-date=2022-11-23}} After that contest ended in a split draw, the organization decided to book Teixeira and Hill, who was scheduled to headline [[UFC Fight Night 221|an event]] against former title challenger [[Anthony Smith (fighter)|Anthony Smith]] in early March.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/11/23/23473784/anthony-smith-vs-jamahal-hill-on-tap-to-headline-march-11-ufc-event|title=Anthony Smith vs. Jamahal Hill on tap to headline March 11 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2022-11-23|access-date=2022-11-23}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/12/dana-white-books-new-light-heavyweight-title-fight-terrible-ufc-282-main-event|title=Dana White's response to 'terrible' UFC 282 headliner? Glover Teixeira vs. Jamahal Hill for vacant title|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun and Ken Hathaway|date=2022-12-11|access-date=2022-12-11}} Smith was expected to serve as an alternate for the title bout.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/12/ufc-283-anthony-smith-serve-backup-glover-hill-vs-jamahal-hill|title=Anthony Smith says he'll fly to Brazil to back up Glover Teixeira vs. Jamahal Hill at UFC 283|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2022-12-19|access-date=2022-12-19}} However, he weighed in at 206.5 pounds on his second attempt and was unable to compete if necessary.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/01/ufc-283-glover-teixeira-jamahal-hill-deiveson-figueiredo-brandon-moreno-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream|title=UFC 283 weigh-in results: Everyone makes weight for first pay-per-view of 2023|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2023-01-20|access-date=2023-01-20}}\n\n{{multiple image\n | align = left\n | total_width = 320\n | image1 = Deiveson Figueiredo 2024.jpg\n | width1 = | height1 = 300\n | alt1 =\n | caption1 =\n | image2 = Brandon Moreno February 2021 (cropped).jpg\n | width2 = | height2 = 300\n | alt2 =\n | caption2 =\n | footer = This was the UFC's first-ever tetralogy fight. [[Deiveson Figueiredo|Figueiredo]] ''(left)'' faced [[Brandon Moreno|Moreno]] ''(right)'' for the fourth straight time, while the latter fought [[Kai Kara-France]] in his latest bout after the original trilogy.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/34721963/deiveson-figueiredo-vs-brandon-moreno-4-set-ufc-283-brazil-sources-say|title=Deiveson Figueiredo vs. Brandon Moreno 4 set for UFC 283 in Brazil, sources say|work=espn.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2022-10-03|access-date=2022-10-04}}}}\n\nA [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] unification tetralogy bout between current two-time champion [[Deiveson Figueiredo]] and former champion/current interim title holder [[Brandon Moreno]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/10/3/23386477/deiveson-figueiredo-vs-brandon-moreno-4-booked-for-ufc-283-in-brazil|title=Deiveson Figueiredo vs. Brandon Moreno 4 booked for UFC 283 in Brazil|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2022-10-03|access-date=2022-10-03}} The pairing first met at [[UFC 256]] on December 12, 2020, which ended in a majority draw (Figueiredo retained the title).{{Cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-256-results-highlights-deiveson-figueiredo-brandon-moreno-fight-to-a-draw-in-an-instant-classic/live/|title=UFC 256 results, highlights: Deiveson Figueiredo, Brandon Moreno fight to a draw in an instant classic|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Brent Brookhouse|date=2021-12-13|access-date=2022-10-03}} Their second meeting took place at [[UFC 263]] on June 12, 2021, where Moreno captured the title by submission in the third round.{{Cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-263-results-highlights-israel-adesanya-retains-title-with-decisive-performance-against-marvin-vettori/live/|title= UFC 263 results, highlights: Israel Adesanya retains title with decisive performance against Marvin Vettori|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Brent Brookhouse|date=2021-07-13|access-date=2022-10-03}} Their third meeting took place at [[UFC 270]] on January 22, 2022, where Figueiredo regained the title by unanimous decision.{{Cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-270-results-highlights-deiveson-figueiredo-edges-brandon-moreno-to-regain-flyweight-championship|title=UFC 270 results, highlights: Deiveson Figueiredo edges Brandon Moreno to regain flyweight championship|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Brent Brookhouse|date=2022-01-23|access-date=2022-10-03}} [[Alexandre Pantoja]] served as backup and potential replacement for this fight.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/10/4/23387864/alexandre-pantoja-backup-fighter-deiveson-figueiredo-brandon-moreno-ufc-283|title=Alexandre Pantoja to serve as backup fighter yet again for Deiveson Figueiredo vs. Brandon Moreno at UFC 283|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2022-10-04|access-date=2022-10-04}}\n\nA women's featherweight bout between [[Josiane Nunes]] and [[Zarah Fairn]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/9/26/23373196/josiane-nunes-zarah-fairn-in-the-works-ufc-283-rio-de-janeiro-brazil|title=Josiane Nunes vs. Zarah Fairn in the works for UFC 283|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2022-09-26|access-date=2022-09-26}} They were originally scheduled to meet at [[UFC on ESPN: Whittaker vs. Gastelum]] in April 2021, but on the day of the weigh-ins Fairn weighed in at 147 pounds, eight pounds over the pre-established limit of 139 pounds (the bout was originally set for the women's bantamweight division, but was shifted to a catchweight prior to the start of the weigh-ins). The bout was cancelled due to the weight discrepancy, as Nunes weighed in at 136 pounds.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/04/ufc-on-espn-22-weigh-ins-video-zarah-fairn-vs-josiane-nunes-weight-miss-11-pounds|title=Zarah Fairn vs. Josiane Nunes scrapped from UFC on ESPN 22 after [[Jailton Almeida]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|date=2022-11-01|title=Jailton Almeida Rebooked Opposite Shamil Abdurakhimov at UFC 283 |url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/11/01/jailton-almeida-rebooked-opposite-shamil-abdurakhimov-jan-14-ufc-event/|access-date=2022-11-03|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson}} They were previously scheduled to meet at [[UFC 279]] and [[UFC 280]] but Abdurakhimov withdrew both times due to visa issues and for undisclosed reasons respectively.{{Cite \n|followingevent= [[UFC on ESPN: Thompson vs. Holland]] \n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Nzechukwu vs. Cuțelaba''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 215''''', '''''UFC on ESPN+ 73''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 65''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on November 19, 2022, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-news-2022-schedule-ends-seven-events-las-vegas|title=NAC approves seven UFC event licenses for remainder of 2022, including six UFC Apex shows|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-09-20|access-date=2022-09-20}}\n\n==Background==\nA heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Derrick Lewis]] and [[Sergey Spivak]] was expected to headline the event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/09/ufc-news-derrick-lewis-vs-serghei-spivac-headlines-nov-19-fight-night-event|title=Derrick Lewis vs. Serghei Spivac to headline Nov. 19 Fight Night event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2022-09-24|access-date=2022-09-24}} However, while the event was in progress, Lewis was forced to withdraw due to non-COVID, non-weight cutting illness and the bout was cancelled. As a result, the intended light heavyweight co-main event between [[Kennedy Nzechukwu]] and [[Ion Cuțelaba]] headlined the event, with the fight being confirmed as the new main event just an hour before the start of the main card.{{Cite web|date=2022-11-19|title=Derrick Lewis reportedly hospitalized, UFC Fight Night 215 headliner vs. Serghei Spivac canceled mid-event|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/11/ufc-news-derrick-lewis-hospitalized-main-event-vs-serghei-spivac-canceled|access-date=2022-11-19 |publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn}}\n\n[[Jack Shore]] was expected to face [[Kyler Phillips]] in a bantamweight bout.{{cite web|url=https://mymmanews.com/jack-shore-vs-kyler-phillips-added-to-upcoming-ufc-fight-night-card/|title=Jack Shore vs. Kyler Phillips added to upcoming UFC Fight Night card|publisher=mymmanews.com|author=Adam Martin|date=2022-08-25|access-date=2022-08-25}} However, Shore suffered \"a serious knee injury\" and is not expect to return to competition before the end of 2022. As a result, Phillips was pulled from the event.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/9/7/23340916/jack-shore-out-of-nov-19-bout-vs-kyler-phillips-cites-serious-knee-injury|title=Jack Shore out of Nov. 19 bout vs. Kyler Phillips, cites 'serious' knee injury|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2022-09-07|access-date=2022-09-07}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[William Knight (fighter)|William Knight]] and [[Marcin Prachnio]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|author=Staff|date=2022-10-13 |title=William Knight announces UFC Fight Night 215 bout vs. Marcin Prachnio |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/10/ufc-fight-night-215-william-knight-vs-marcin-prachnio-mma-matchup-booking-announcement|access-date=2022-10-13|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com}} However, the bout was scrapped a week before the event for undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmarocks.pl/mma-news/walka-z-udzialem-marcina-prachnio-odwolana-na-tydzien-przed-gala|title=The fight with the participation of Marcin Prachnio canceled a week before the gala|publisher=mmarocks.pl|author=Michał Tkaczuk|date=2022-11-11|access-date=2022-11-17}} {{in lang|pl}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Rodolfo Vieira]] and [[Cody Brundage]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mma.uno/cody-brundage-vs-rodolfo-vieira-agregado-a-ufc-fight-night-del-19-de-noviembre|title=Cody Brundage vs. Rodolfo Vieira added to UFC Fight Night on November 19|publisher=mma.uno|author=Steve Duncan|date=2022-08-25|access-date=2022-08-25}} {{in lang|es}} However, Vieira pulled out from the event due to undisclosed reasons and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://www.itnwwe.com/mma/rodolfo-veira-vs-cody-brundage-cancelled-ufc-vegas-65/|title=Rodolfo Vieira pulls out of UFC Vegas 65, Cody Brundage fight off|publisher=itnwwe.com|author=Vipin Raghuwanshi|date=2022-11-15|access-date=2022-11-15}}\n\n[[Vince Morales]] was expected to face José Johnson in a bantamweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2022/9/27/23375514/vince-morales-takes-on-recent-contender-series-winner-jose-johnson-at-ufc-vegas-65-mma|title=Vince Morales set to take on recent Contender Series winner Jose Johnson at UFC Vegas 65|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Drake Riggs|date=2022-09-27|access-date=2022-09-27}} However, Johnson pulled out for undisclosed reasons and was replaced by former [[Legacy_Fighting_Alliance#LFA_Bantamweight_Championship|LFA Bantamweight Champion]] [[Miles Johns]].{{Cite web|url=https://mymmanews.com/miles-johns-talks-short-notice-opportunity-at-ufc-vegas-65/|title=Miles Johns talks short notice opportunity at UFC Vegas 65|publisher=mymmanews.com|author=John Eric Poli|date=2022-11-11|access-date=2022-11-18}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (ESPN+)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Kennedy Nzechukwu]]\n|def.\n|[[Ion [[UFC Fight Night: Cannonier vs. Strickland]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC 283|UFC 283: Teixeira vs. Hill]] \n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Strickland vs. Imavov''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 217''''', '''''UFC on ESPN+ 75''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 67''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on January 14, 2023, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|date=2022-09-28|title=Kevin Gastelum vs. Nassourdine Imavov headlines first UFC event of 2023|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/09/ufc-news-kevin-gastelum-vs-nassourdine-imavov-headlines-fight-night-january-2023|access-date=2022-09-29|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn and Farah Hannoun}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-news-2023-schedule-san-antonio-korea-las-vegas|title=UFC officially announces early 2023 schedule: Dates, locations, and venues|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-12-08|access-date=2022-12-09}}\n\n==Background==\nA middleweight bout between former interim [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] challenger and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen]] middleweight winner [[Kelvin Gastelum]] and [[Nassourdine Imavov]] was scheduled to headline the event.{{Cite web|author=Damon Martin |date=2022-09-28|title=Kelvin Gastelum vs. Nassourdine Imavov expected to headline first UFC card in 2023|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/9/28/23377326/kelvin-gastelum-vs-nassourdine-imavov-expected-to-headline-first-ufc-card-in-2023|access-date=2022-09-29|publisher=mmafighting.com}} The pair was previously scheduled to meet at [[UFC 273]], but Imavov was forced to withdraw due to visa issues.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/03/ufc-273-news-nassourdine-imavov-vs-kelvin-gastelum-off-ilir-latifi-vs-aleksei-oleinik-added|title=UFC 273 news: Nassourdine Imavov vs. Kelvin Gastelum off, Ilir Latifi vs. Aleksei Oleinik added|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2022-03-28|access-date=2022-03-28}} However, the week of the event, Gastelum withdrew due to a mouth injury and was replaced by [[Sean Strickland]], with the bout taking place at light heavyweight.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/1/9/23547403/kelvin-gastelum-out-sean-strickland-now-faces-nassourdine-imavov-in-ufc-vegas-67-main-event|title=Kelvin Gastelum out, Sean Strickland now faces Nassourdine Imavov in UFC Vegas 67 main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2023-01-09|access-date=2023-01-09}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Jailton Almeida]] and [[Shamil Abdurakhimov]] was originally announced for this date. However the UFC issued a correction a short time later announcing the fight was set for [[UFC 283]] the following week.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/11/01/jailton-almeida-rebooked-opposite-shamil-abdurakhimov-jan-14-ufc-event/|title=Jailton Almeida Rebooked Opposite Shamil Abdurakhimov at UFC 283|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2022-11-01|access-date=2022-11-02}}\n\nA flyweight bout between [[Jeff Molina]] and Jimmy Flick was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.eurosport.nl/mixed-martial-arts/ufc/2022/ufc-molina-vs.-flick-eerste-gevecht-voor-2023-op-14-januari_sto9157860/story.shtml|title=UFC {{!}} Molina vs. Flick eerste gevecht voor 2023 op 14 januari|publisher=eurosport.nl|author=Marcel Droff|date=2022-09-24|access-date=2022-09-29}} {{in lang|nl}} However, Molina withdrew from the event due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by [[Charles Johnson (fighter)|Charles Johnson]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/12/5/23495552/charles-johnson-in-for-jeff-molina-at-ufc-vegas-67-faces-jimmy-flick|title=Charles Johnson in for Jeff Molina at UFC Vegas 67, faces Jimmy Flick|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck and MMA Fighting Newswire|date=2022-12-05|access-date=2022-12-06}} A few weeks later it was announced that Molina was under suspension by the [[Nevada Athletic Commission]] as he has been linked to an investigation around the [[UFC Fight Night: Rodriguez vs. Lemos#Betting controversy|Nov. 5 fight between Darrick Minner and Shayilan Nuerdanbieke]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/12/ufc-betting-scandal-jeff-molina-suspension-nevada-james-krause-fbi-investigation|title=Report: UFC flyweight Jeff Molina the latest suspension amid betting scandal|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2022-12-26|access-date=2022-12-26}}\n\nWelterweights [[Geoff Neal]] and [[Shavkat Rakhmonov]] were expected to meet at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Welterweights-Geoff-Neal-Shavkat-Rakhmonov-to-Clash-at-UFC-Fight-Night-on-Jan-14-188159|title=Welterweights Geoff Neal, Shavkat Rakhmonov to clash at UFC Fight Night on Jan. 14|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2022-10-08|access-date=2022-10-10}} However on December 29, Neal pulled out due to undisclosed injury and the bout was rescheduled for [[UFC 285]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportskeeda.com/mma/geoff-neal-scheduled-bout-shavkat-rakhmonov-14th-january-2023|title=Geoff Neal out of scheduled bout against Fight Night: Andrade vs. Blanchfield]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 284: Makhachev vs. Volkanovski''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on 12 February 2023, at [[RAC Arena]] in [[Perth]], Australia.{{Cite web|url=https://www.perthnow.com.au/news/wa/ufc-284-mma-promotion-to-return-to-perth-for-the-first-time-in-four-years-with-february-event--c-8477710|title=UFC 284: MMA promotion to return to Perth for the first time in four years with February event|publisher=perthnow.com.au|author=Josh Zimmerman and Bryce Luff|date=2022-10-07|access-date=2022-10-07}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's 16th overall trip to Australia, returning to the country for the first time since [[UFC 243]] in [[Melbourne]] in October 2019.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/10/ufc-284-perth-australia-february-2023-mma-fight-booking-announcement|title=Report: UFC 284 set for Australia return in Perth in February|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2022-10-07|access-date=2022-10-07}}\n\n{{multiple image\n | align = left\n | total_width = 360\n | image1 = Islam Makhachev 2022 UFC belt.png\n | width1 = 2600 | height1 = 2200\n | alt1 =\n | caption1 =\n | image2 = Alexander Volkanovski at UFC 232.jpg\n | width2 = 2300 | height2 = 2200\n | alt2 =\n | caption2 =\n | footer = Makhachev ''(left)'' made his first title defense after winning the title at [[UFC 280]]. If victorious, Volkanovski ''(right)'' would have become the second man to be a simultaneous UFC featherweight and lightweight champion.\n}}\n\nA [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Islam Makhachev]] and current [[UFC Featherweight Championship|UFC Featherweight Champion]] [[Alexander Volkanovski]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/11/12/23455198/islam-makhachev-vs-alexander-volkanovski-lightweight-title-fight-official-for-ufc-284|title=Islam Makhachev vs. Alexander Volkanovski lightweight title fight official for UFC 284|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2022-11-12|access-date=2022-11-12}} This was the eighth time in UFC history that champions in different divisions fought for the same title, following [[UFC 94]], [[UFC 205]], [[UFC 226]], [[UFC 232]], [[UFC Fight Night: Cejudo vs. Dillashaw]], [[UFC 259]], and [[UFC 277]]. If successful, Volkanovski would have become the fourth man to be champion in two divisions simultaneously and fifth fighter overall (after [[Conor McGregor]] at [[UFC 205]], [[Daniel Cormier]] at [[UFC 226]], [[Amanda Nunes]] at [[UFC 232]] and [[UFC 277]], and [[Henry Cejudo]] at [[UFC 238]]), as well as the eighth person overall to win a title in different divisions.{{Cite web|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/ufc-featherweight-champion-alex-volkanovski-to-face-lightweight-champ-islam-makhachev-for-dual-belt-status-230453781.html|title=UFC featherweight champion Alex Volkanovski to face lightweight champ Islam Makhachev for dual-belt status|publisher=sports.yahoo.com|author=Jack Baer|date=2022-11-12|access-date=2022-11-14}} It was also the first time in the organization's history that the number 1 pound-for-pound fighter (Volkanovski) faced the number 2 fighter (Makhachev) for a championship.{{Cite web|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/ufc-284-alexander-volkanovski-islam-makhachev-to-make-history-in-first-no-1-vs-no-2-matchup-202223669.html|title=UFC 284: Alexander Volkanovski, Islam Makhachev to make history in first No. 1 vs. No. 2 matchup|publisher=sports.yahoo.com|author=Kevin Iole|date=2023-02-06|access-date=2023-02-10}}\n\nAn interim [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] bout between [[The Ultimate Fighter: Latin America]] featherweight winner [[Yair Rodríguez]] and [[Josh Emmett]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2022/11/13/23455814/yair-rodriguez-vs-josh-emmett-interim-featherweight-title-fight-added-to-ufc-284-ppv-australia|title=Yair Rodriguez vs. Josh Emmett interim featherweight title fight added to UFC 284|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Dan Hiergesell|date=2022-11-13|access-date=2022-11-13}}\n\nA middleweight bout between former [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: The Smashes]] welterweight winner) [[Robert Whittaker (fighter)|Robert Whittaker]] and former title challenger [[Paulo Costa]] was linked to this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/10/20/23415312/robert-whittaker-vs-paulo-costa-booked-for-ufc-284-in-australia|title=Robert Whittaker vs. Paulo Costa booked for UFC 284 in Australia|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2022-10-20|access-date=2022-10-20}} However, Costa disputed the official announcement by the promotion indicating he had never signed a contract and the fight would not take place.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/12/ufc-284-paulo-costa-vs-robert-whittaker-sell-tickets|title='They should stop being 2021, but Costa withdrew due to illness.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/3/16/22333374/paulo-costa-ill-out-ufc-fight-robert-whittaker-mma|title=Paulo Costa ill, unable to fight Robert Whittaker at April 17 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2021-03-16|access-date=2021-10-20}}\n\nA flyweight bout between former [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challengers [[Alex Perez (fighter)|Alex Perez]] and [[Kai Kara-France]] (the latter for the interim title) was scheduled for this event.{{Cite was moved to a different location due to the [[COVID-19 pandemic]] and the bout eventually never took place.{{Cite Fight Night: Lewis vs. Spivak]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/11/ufc-books-loma-lookboonmee-vs-elise-reed-feb-4-south-korea|title=UFC books Loma Lookboonmee vs. Elise Reed for event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.abc.es/deportes/joel-alvarez-peleara-jamie-mullarkey-australia-20221108141417-nt.html|title=Joel Álvarez will fight Zubaira Tukhugov at UFC 284 in \n|followingevent= [[UFC 284|UFC 284: Makhachev vs. Volkanovski]] \n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Spivac''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 218''''', '''''UFC on ESPN+ 76''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 68''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on February 4, 2023, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/12/15/23511155/derrick-lewis-vs-serghei-spivac-rebooked-to-headline-ufc-vegas-68|title=Derrick Lewis vs. Sergey Spivak rebooked to headline UFC Vegas 68|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2022-12-15|access-date=2022-12-15}}\n\n==Background==\nThe organization originally targeted a return to [[Seoul]], [[South Korea]] to host the event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/10/19/first-fights-reported-ufc-seoul-feb-24-2023/|title=First fights reported for UFC Seoul on Feb. 24, 2023|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2022-10-19|access-date=2022-12-15}} Instead, the event was moved to the [[UFC Apex]] in [[Enterprise, Nevada]].\n\nA heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Derrick Lewis]] and [[Serghei Spivac]] headlined the event. The pair was previously expected to headline [[UFC Fight Night: Nzechukwu vs. Cuțelaba]] in November 2022, but while the event was in progress, Lewis was forced to withdraw due to non-COVID, non-weight-cutting illness, and the bout was cancelled.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/11/ufc-news-derrick-lewis-hospitalized-main-event-vs-serghei-spivac-canceled|title=Derrick Lewis reportedly hospitalized, UFC Fight Night 215 headliner vs. Sergey Spivak canceled mid-event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2022-11-22|access-date=2022-12-15}} However, Lewis later confirmed that he had, in fact, contracted [[COVID-19]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/02/derrick-lewis-covid-19-reason-why-original-booking-vs-serghei-spivac-fell-through|title=Derrick Lewis: COVID-19 reason why original booking vs. Serghei Spivac fell through|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2023-02-01|access-date=2023-02-02}}\n\nA women's Strawweight bout between [[Loma Lookboonmee]] and [[Elise Reed]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/11/ufc-books-loma-lookboonmee-vs-elise-reed-feb-4-south-korea|title=UFC books Loma Lookboonmee vs. Elise Reed for Feb. 4 main event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/02/ufc-fight-night-220-news-nikita-krylov-illness-cancels-main-event-vs-ryan-spann|title=Nikita Krylov out of UFC Fight Night 220 main Yan vs. Dvalishvili]] two weeks later.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/2023/02/nikita-krylov-vs-ryan-spann-rebooked-loses-main-event-status/|title=Nikita Krylov vs. Ryan Spann reportedly withdrew from the event due to undisclosed reasons.{{cite Tognoni.{{Cite web|url=https://www.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2023/03/04/jake-gyllenhaal-road-house-remake-scene-ufc-285-weigh-ins/11402490002/|title=Jake Gyllenhaal slap at UFC 285 weigh-ins for by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on March 25, 2023, at the [[AT&T Center]] in [[San Antonio, Texas]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/12/08/ufc-heading-san-antonio-march-25-fight-night-event/|title=UFC heading to San Antonio for March 25, 2023 Fight Night event|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2022-12-08|access-date=2022-12-08}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-news-2023-schedule-san-antonio-korea-las-vegas|title=UFC officially announces early 2023 schedule: Dates, locations, and venues|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-12-08|access-date=2022-12-09}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's third visit to San Antonio and first since [[UFC on ESPN: dos Anjos vs. Edwards]] in July 2019.\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Marlon Vera]] and former interim [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Cory Sandhagen]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/2/2/23583803/cory-sandhagen-vs-marlon-vera-rebooked-for-march-25-ufc-event|title=Cory Sandhagen vs. Marlon Vera rebooked to headline March 25 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Staff|date=2023-02-02|access-date=2023-02-02}} They were previously expected to headline [[UFC Fight Night: Andrade vs. Blanchfield]], but were rescheduled to this event for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/12/ufc-news-marlon-chito-vera-vs-cory-sandhagen-booked-feb-18-headliner|title=Marlon Vera vs. Cory Sandhagen to headline UFC Fight Night on Feb. 18|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2022-12-23|access-date=2023-02-02}}\n\nAlthough it was not announced by the promotion, a rematch between [[Irene Aldana]] and former [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Raquel Pennington]] was originally rumored to headline this event.{{Cite web|author=Vipin Raghuwanshi|date=2023-01-26|title=Irene Aldana vs Raquel Pennington 2 to Headline UFC San Antonio|url=https://www.itnwwe.com/mma/irene-aldana-vs-raquel-pennington-2-headline-ufc-san-antonio/|access-date=2023-01-27|publisher=itnwwe.com}} The pair previously met at [[UFC on ESPN: dos Anjos vs. Edwards]], where Pennington won by a split decision.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2019/07/20/ufc-san-antonio-results-raquel-pennington-snaps-skid-win-irene-aldana/|title=UFC San Antonio results: Raquel Pennington snaps Skid with split-decision win over Irene Aldana|author=Heath Harshman|date=2019-07-20|publisher=cagesidepress.com|access-date=2023-02-02}} However, the bout never materialized.\n\n[[Sean Brady (fighter)|Sean Brady]] and [[Michel Pereira]] were expected to meet in a welterweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2022/12/26/23527380/sean-brady-vs-michel-pereira-on-tap-for-ufc-san-antonio-in-march-mma|title=Sean Brady vs. Michel Pereira on tap for UFC San Antonio in March|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Drake Riggs|date=2022-12-26|access-date=2022-12-26}} However, Brady pulled out in mid-February due to a torn groin and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/02/ufc-on-espn-43-sean-brady-says-he-tore-groin-michel-pereira-fight-off|title=Sean Brady says he tore groin; UFC on ESPN 43 fight with Michel Pereira off|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2023-02-11|access-date=2023-02-11}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Alex Caceres]] and [[Nate Landwehr]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/01/ufc-news-alex-caceres-vs-nate-landwehr-san-antonio|title=UFC adds Alex Caceres vs. Nate Landwehr to San Antonio lineup|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2023-01-12|access-date=2023-01-12}} However, Caceres withdrew due to an undisclosed reason and was replaced by [[Austin Lingo]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmarocks.pl/ufc/nate-landwehr-zmierzy-sie-z-austinem-lingo-na-ufc-san-antonio|title=Nate Landwehr will face Austin Lingo at UFC San Antonio|publisher=mmarocks.pl|author=Hubert Zdun|date=2023-03-13|access-date=2023-03-15}} {{in lang|pl}}\n\nLiang Na and [[Brogan Walker-Sanchez]] were expected to meet in a women's flyweight bout.{{Cite Antonio|publisher=asianmma.com|author=Staff|date=2023-02-10|access-date=2023-02-10}} However, Liang pulled out due to undisclosed reasons and Walker-Sanchez was moved to [[UFC Fight Night: Pavlovich vs. Blaydes]] a month later as a replacement against Iasmin Lucindo.{{Cite web|url=https://www.itnwwe.com/mma/brogan-walker-vs-iasmin-lucindo-april-22-ufc/|title=Brogan Walker replaced M.Martinez against Iasmin Lucindo at April 22 UFC event|publisher=itnwwe.com|author=Arshad|date=2023-03-17|access-date=2023-03-18}}\n\nTamires Vidal was expected to face Hailey Cowan in a women's bantamweight bout at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/03/ufc-san-antonio-fight-card-hailey-cowan-vs-tamires-vidal|title=UFC on ESPN 43 gets late addition: Hailey Cowan vs. Tamires Vidal scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/03/ufc-on-espn-43-loses-hailey-cowan-vs-tamires-vidal-undisclosed-medical-issue|title=UFC on ESPN 43 news: Tamires Vidal out, Hailey Cowan's Lee|date=2022-12-07|access-date=2022-12-07}}\n\n== Background ==\nA [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] trilogy bout between current champion [[Leon Edwards]] and former champion (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: American Top Team vs. Blackzilians]] welterweight winner) [[Kamaru Usman]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/1/14/23498754/leon-edwards-vs-kamaru-usman-3-set-for-ufc-286-in-london|title=Leon Edwards vs. Kamaru Usman 3 set for UFC 286 in London|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2023-01-14|access-date=2023-01-14}} The pairing first met at [[UFC on Fox: dos Anjos vs. Cowboy 2]] in December 2015, where Usman won by unanimous decision.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/12/ufc-on-fox-17-results-kamaru-usman-grinds-out-leon-edwards|title=UFC on FOX 17 results: Kamaru Usman grinds out Leon Edwards|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2015-12-19|access-date=2015-12-19|archive-date=2018-06-24|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180624092908/https://mmajunkie.com/2015/12/ufc-on-fox-17-results-kamaru-usman-grinds-out-leon-edwards|url-status=live}} Their second meeting took place at [[UFC 278]] in August 2022, where Edwards won the title by knockout in the fifth round.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/34433821/leon-edwards-takes-kamaru-usman-ufc-title-5th-round-head-kick-ko|title=Edwards shocks Usman with late head-kick KO|work=espn.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2022-08-21|access-date=2022-08-22}} Former interim champion [[Colby Covington]] served as back up and potential replacement for this fight.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/03/ufc-286-london-video-colby-covington-confirmed-backup-welterweight-title-fight-weigh-in-highlights|title=Colby Covington confirmed as backup for UFC 286 main event welterweight title fight|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matt Erickson and Abbey Subhan|date=2023-03-17|access-date=2023-03-17}}\n\nA lightweight bout between Michał Figlak and [[Chris Duncan (fighter)|Chris Duncan]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmarocks.pl/ufc-286/michal-figlak-kontra-chris-duncan-na-gali-ufc-286-w-londynie|title=Michał Figlak vs Chris Duncan at UFC 286 in London|publisher=mmarocks.pl|author=Tomasz Nowosielski|date=2023-01-11|access-date=2023-02-08}} {{in lang|pl}} However, Figlak was forced to withdraw from the event citing injury and was replaced by [[Omar Morales (fighter)|Omar Morales]].{{Cite web|author=Arshad|date=2023-02-08|title=UFC 286: Omar Morales to replace Michal Figlak against Chris Duncan|url=https://www.itnwwe.com/mma/omar-morales-vs-chris-duncan-set-for-ufc-286/|access-date=2023-02-08|publisher=itnwwe.com}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Nathaniel Wood (fighter)|Nathaniel
