\n|followingevent= [[UFC 286|UFC 286: Edwards vs. Usman 3]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Yan vs. Dvalishvili''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 221''''', '''''UFC on ESPN+ 79''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on March 11, 2023, at [[The Theater at Virgin Hotels]] in [[Paradise, Nevada]] part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/12/08/non-apex-las-vegas-ufc-fight-night-set-march-11-theater-virgin-hotels/|title=Non-Apex Las Vegas UFC Fight Night set for March 11 at The Theater at Virgin Hotels|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2022-12-08|access-date=2022-12-08}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-news-2023-schedule-san-antonio-korea-las-vegas|title=UFC officially announces early 2023 schedule: Dates, locations, and venues|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-12-08|access-date=2022-12-09}}\n\n==Background==\nThe venue was originally known as The Joint (part of the [[Hard Rock Hotel and Casino (Las Vegas)|Hard Rock Hotel and Casino]]) and held several UFC and [[World Extreme Cagefighting|WEC]] events in the early 2000s. This event marked the promotion's first visit to the venue after it was rebranded and the first since [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Carwin vs. Team Nelson#The Ultimate Fighter 16 Finale|The Ultimate Fighter: Team Carwin vs. Team Nelson Finale]] in December 2012.\n\nA light heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Anthony Smith (fighter)|Anthony Smith]] and [[Jamahal Hill]] was expected to headline the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/11/23/23473784/anthony-smith-vs-jamahal-hill-on-tap-to-headline-march-11-ufc-event|title=Anthony Smith vs. Jamahal Hill on tap to headline March 11 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2022-11-23|access-date=2022-11-23}} However, in mid-December, Hill was pulled from the contest in order to headline [[UFC 283]] against former champion [[Glover Teixeira]] for the vacant title.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/12/dana-white-books-new-light-heavyweight-title-fight-terrible-ufc-282-main-event|title=Dana White's response to 'terrible' UFC 282 headliner? Glover Teixeira vs. Jamahal Hill for vacant title|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun and Ken Hathaway|date=2022-12-11|access-date=2022-12-11}} Smith was later scheduled to face [[Johnny Walker (fighter)|Johnny Walker]] on May 13 at [[UFC on ABC: Rozenstruik vs. Almeida]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/2/10/23593485/anthony-smith-vs-johnny-walker-headlines-ufc-event-on-may-13|title=Anthony Smith vs. Johnny Walker headlines UFC event on May 13|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2023-02-10|access-date=2023-02-10}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between former [[List of UFC champions#Bantamweight Championship|UFC Bantamweight Champion]] [[Petr Yan]] and [[Merab Dvalishvili]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/01/ufc-fight-night-221-merab-dvalishvili-vs-petr-yan-main-event|title=Petr Yan vs. Merab Dvalishvili headlines UFC Fight Night 221|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2023-01-17|access-date=2023-01-17}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Mario Bautista]] and [[Guido Cannetti]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/12/ufc-news-guido-cannetti-vs-mario-bautista-march-fight-night|title=UFC books Mario Bautista vs. Guido Cannetti for March event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2022-12-16|access-date=2022-12-16}} They were previously scheduled to meet at [[UFC on ESPN: Barboza vs. Chikadze]], but Bautista pulled out after [[COVID-19 testing|testing positive for COVID-19]].{{Cite web|date=2021-08-17|title=Sara McMann highlights three late withdrawals from UFC on ESPN 30|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/08/ufc-on-espn-30-sara-mcmann-three-withdrawals|access-date=2021-08-21|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Makhmud Muradov]] and [[Abusupiyan Magomedov]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|author=Steve Duncan|date=2023-01-31|title=Makhmud Muradov vs. Abusupiyan Magomedov added to UFC Vegas 71|url=https://mma.uno/makhmud-muradov-vs-abusupiyan-magomedov-agregado-a-ufc-vegas-71/|access-date=2023-01-31|publisher=mma.uno}} {{in lang|es}} They were previously scheduled to meet at [[UFC Fight Night: Gane vs. Tuivasa]] but Muradov withdrew due to injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/7/28/23281677/ufc-fight-roundup-hakeem-dawodu-vs-julian-erosa-jake-collier-vs-chris-barnett-set-for-ufc-279|title=UFC Fight Roundup: Hakeem Dawodu vs. Julian Erosa, Jake Collier vs. Chris Barnett set for UFC 279|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2022-07-28|access-date=2022-08-17}} At the end of February, it was announced that Muradov had pulled out of the bout again.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmarocks.pl/abus-magomedov/makhmud-muradov-wypada-z-marcowej-gali-ufc-abus-magomedov-kolejny-raz-bez-rywala|title=Makhmud Muradov wypada z marcowej gali UFC. Abus Magomedov kolejny raz bez rywala|publisher=mmarocks.pl|author=Bartłomiej Zubkiewicz |date=2023-02-26|access-date=2023-03-05}} {{in lang|pl}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Raphael Assunção]] and [[Kyler Phillips]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Raphael-Assuncao-to-Face-Kyler-Phillips-at-UFC-Fight-Night-221-on-March-11-188835|title=Raphael Assuncao to Face Kyler Phillips at UFC Fight Night 221 on March 11|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2023-01-02|access-date=2023-01-02}} However, Phillips withdrew from the event for undisclosed reasons and was replaced by [[Davey Grant]].{{Cite web|author=Steve Duncan|date=2023-02-10|title=Phillips out, Davey Grant faces Raphael Assuncao en UFC Vegas 71|url=https://mma.uno/phillips-fuera-davey-grant-enfrenta-a-raphael-assuncao-en-ufc-vegas-71/|access-date=2023-02-11|publisher=mma.uno}} {{in lang|es}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Nikita Krylov]] and former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Light Heavyweight Championship|LFA Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Ryan Spann]] was originally expected to headline [[UFC Fight Night: Muniz vs. Allen]].{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/11/24/ryan-spann-vs-nikita-krylov-headlines-ufcs-feb-25-event/|title=Ryan Spann vs. Nikita Krylov headlines UFC's Feb. 25 event|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2022-11-24|access-date=2022-11-24}} However, the headliner was cancelled during the broadcast as Krylov had fallen ill. The pair was rescheduled for this event at a catchweight of 215 pounds.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/2023/02/nikita-krylov-vs-ryan-spann-rebooked-loses-main-event-status/|title=Nikita Krylov vs. Ryan Spann reportedly rebooked but loses main event status|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Harvey Leonard|date=2023-02-27|access-date=2023-02-27}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/03/ufc-fight-night-221-nikita-krylov-details-fight-day-illness-canceled-ryan-spann-main-event|title=Nikita Krylov details fight-day illness that forced him out of main event vs. Ryan Spann|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells and Ken Hathaway|date=2023-03-08|access-date=2023-03-09}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Carlston Harris]] and [[Abubakar Nurmagomedov]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mma.uno/carlston-harris-vs-abubakar-nurmagomedov-agregado-a-ufc-vegas-71/|title=Carlston Harris vs. Abubakar Nurmagomedov added to UFC Vegas 71|publisher=mma.uno|author=Steve Duncan|date=2023-02-06|access-date=2023-02-07}} {{in lang|es}} However, Nurmagomedov withdrew from the bout due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by [[Jared Gooden]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/3/8/23631140/jared-gooden-steps-in-abubakar-nurmagomedov-carlston-harris-ufc-las-vegas|title=Jared Gooden steps in for Abubakar Nurmagomedov vs. Carlston Harris at UFC Las Vegas|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2023-03-08|access-date=2023-03-08}} At the weigh-ins, Gooden weighed in at 177 pounds, six pounds over the welterweight non-title fight limit. The bout proceeded at catchweight and Gooden was fined 30% of his purse, which went to Harris.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/03/ufc-fight-night-221-video-las-vegas-petr-yan-merab-dvalishvili-weigh-in-results-live-stream|title=UFC Fight Night 221 weigh-in results: Two massive weight misses lead to one canceled fight|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2023-03-10|access-date=2023-03-10}}\n\nAlso at the weigh-ins, [[Ricardo Ramos (fighter)|Ricardo Ramos]] weighed in at 154 pounds, eight pounds over the featherweight non-title fight limit. As a result, his bout with [[Austin Lingo]] was cancelled.\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (ESPN+)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Bantamweight\n|[[Merab Dvalishvili]]\n|def.\n|[[Petr \n|followingevent= [[UFC on ESPN: Holloway vs. Allen]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 287: Pereira vs. Adesanya 2''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on April 8, 2023, at the [[Kaseya Center]] in [[Miami, Florida]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/2/4/23585719/ufc-announces-miami-location-pereira-adesanya-masvidal-burns|title=UFC 287 headed to Miami for Pereira vs. Adesanya 2, Masvidal vs. Burns|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2023-02-04|access-date=2023-02-04}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's second visit to Miami and first since [[UFC 42]] in April 2003.\n\nA [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] rematch between current champion [[Alex Pereira]] (also former [[Glory (kickboxing)#Glory Middleweight Championship|Glory Middleweight]] and [[Glory (kickboxing)#Glory Light Heavyweight Championship|Light Heavyweight Champion]]) and former champion [[Israel Adesanya]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/01/27/ufc-287-gets-pereira-vs-adesanya-2-burns-vs-masvidal/|title=UFC 287 gets Pereira vs. Adesanya 2, Burns vs. Masvidal, Holland vs. Ponzinibbio|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2023-01-27|access-date=2023-01-27}} The pairing previously met at [[UFC 281]] in which Pereira captured the title by fifth-round knockout.{{Cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-281-results-takeaways-the-magic-of-madison-square-garden-delivers-again-in-an-all-time-event/amp/|title=UFC 281 results, takeaways: The magic of Madison Square Garden delivers again in an all-time event|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Brian Campbell|date=2022-11-13|access-date=2023-01-27}} The two also previously met twice in kickboxing matchups: the first in April 2016 at [[2016 in Glory of Heroes#Glory of Heroes 1|Glory of Heroes 1]], which Pereira won by unanimous decision, and their second at [[2017 in Glory of Heroes|Glory of Heroes 7]] in March 2017, which Pereira won by third-round knockout.{{Cite web|url=https://mmauk.net/2020/11/25/alex-pereira-and-israel-adesanya-an-undeniable-trilogy/|title=Alex Pereira and Israel Adesanya: an undeniable trilogy?|publisher=mmauk.net|author=Michael Owens|date=2020-11-25|access-date=2022-08-05}}\n\nNikolas Motta was expected to face [[Ignacio Bahamondes]] in a lightweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/01/ignacio-bahamondes-vs-nikolas-motta-ufc-287-april-8-mma-fight-booking-matchup-announcement|title=Ignacio Bahamondes vs. Nikolas Motta on tap for UFC 287 in April|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2023-01-18|access-date=2023-03-26}} However, Motta pulled out in late March due to a cut on his head.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/news/nikolas-motta-nasty-head-injury-ufc-287-withdrawal|title=Nikolas Motta withdraws from UFC 287 after brutal headbutt accident|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Curtis Calhoun|date=2023-03-24|access-date=2023-03-26}} He was replaced by Trey Ogden at a catchweight of 160 pounds.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/03/ufc-287-ignacio-bahamondes-vs-trey-ogden-mma-booking-matchup-nikolas-motta-out|title=With Nikolas Motta out, Ignacio Bahamondes now will face Trey Ogden at UFC 287|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun and Nolan King|date=2023-03-28|access-date=2023-03-28}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[The Ultimate Fighter: Live]] lightweight winner [[Michael Chiesa]] and [[Li Jingliang]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Michael-ChiesaJingliang-Li-Added-to-UFC-287-Lineup-on-April-8-189089|title=Michael Chiesa-Jingliang Li added to UFC 287 lineup on April 8|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pretty|date=2023-02-04|access-date=2023-02-04}} However, Li withdrew from the bout due to a back injury and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/03/ufc-287-news-li-jingliang-vs-michael-chiesa-injury|title=Li Jingliang out of UFC 287 fight vs. Michael Chiesa due to injury|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2023-03-27|access-date=2023-03-27}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/04/ufc-287-fight-card-news-michael-chiesa-out-li-jingliang|title=Michael Chiesa announces he's off UFC 287 after replacement options fizzle|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2023-04-03|access-date=2023-04-03}}\n\n[[Chris Barnett]] was expected to face [[Chase Sherman]] in a heavyweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2023/2/15/23600865/ufc-287-adds-chris-barnett-vs-chase-sherman-heavyweight-scrap|title=UFC 287 fight card: Chris Barnett vs. Chase Sherman heavyweight scrap headed to Miami|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Ryan Harkness|date=2023-02-15|access-date=2023-04-02}} However, Barnett pulled out due to undisclosed reasons in early April and was replaced by Karl Williams.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportskeeda.com/mma/news-ufc-287-loses-heavyweight-matchup-new-opponent-s-name-reported|title=UFC 287 loses heavyweight matchup, new opponent's name reported|publisher=sportskeeda.com|author=Umar|date=2023-04-02|access-date=2023-04-02}} In turn, Sherman withdrew the day of the event due to a medical issue and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/platform/amp/2023/4/8/23675723/karl-williams-vs-chase-sherman-pulled-from-ufc-287|title=Karl Williams vs. Chase Sherman pulled from UFC 287|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2023-04-08|access-date=2023-04-08}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Christian Rodriguez weighed in at 137 pounds, one pound over the bantamweight non-title fight limit. His bout proceeded at catchweight and he was fined 20% of his purse which went to his opponent [[Raul Rosas Jr.]]{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/platform/amp/2023/4/7/23674119/ufc-287-weigh-in-results-alex-pereira-israel-adesanya-main-event-grudge-match-fighter-misses-weight|title=UFC 287 weigh-in results: Alex Pereira, Israel Adesanya set for main event grudge match, one fighter misses weight|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2023-04-07|access-date=2023-04-07}}\n\nDuring the event's broadcast, it was announced that a [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] bout that took place in July 2015 at [[UFC 189]] between then champion [[Robbie Lawler]] and challenger [[Rory MacDonald (fighter)|Rory MacDonald]] was the next \"fight wing\" [[UFC Hall of Fame]] inductee. Lawler defended the title via fifth-round TKO. It was also a rematch of a November 2013 fight in which Lawler also won (via split decision).{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/4/9/23675928/robbie-lawler-vs-rory-macdonald-clash-at-ufc-189-announced-for-ufc-hall-of-fame|title=Robbie Lawler vs. Rory MacDonald clash at UFC 189 announced for UFC Hall of Fame|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. [[UFC Fight Night: Dern vs. Hill]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on ABC: Rozenstruik vs. Almeida''''' (also known as '''''UFC on ABC 4''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on May 13, 2023, at the [[Spectrum Center (arena)|Spectrum Center]] in [[Charlotte, North Carolina]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/03/ufc-charlotte-fight-night-may-13-north-carolina-event-announcement-anthony-smith-vs-johnny-walker|title=UFC's May 13 Fight Night event lands in Charlotte|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2023-03-30|access-date=2023-03-30}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's fifth visit to Charlotte and first since [[UFC on Fox: Jacaré vs. Brunson 2]] in January 2018.\n\nA light heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Anthony Smith (fighter)|Anthony Smith]] and [[Johnny Walker (fighter)|Johnny Walker]] was originally expected to headline the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/2/10/23593485/anthony-smith-vs-johnny-walker-headlines-ufc-event-on-may-13|title=Anthony Smith vs. Johnny Walker headlines UFC event on May 13|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2023-02-10|access-date=2023-02-10}} However, the UFC decided to promote an already scheduled three-round heavyweight bout between [[Jairzinho Rozenstruik]] and [[Jailton Almeida]] to the five-round main event spot and downgrade the previous headliner to a three-round co-main event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/2/20/23607424/jailton-almeida-to-fight-jairzinho-rozenstruik-at-may-13-ufc-event|title=Jailton Almeida to fight Jairzinho Rozenstruik at May 13 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2023-02-20|access-date=2023-02-20}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/04/ufc-charlotte-news-jairzinho-rozenstruik-vs-jailton-almeida-johnny-walker-anthony-smith|title=UFC Charlotte upgrades undercard fight to new main event; downgrades previous headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Mike Bohn|date=2023-04-04|access-date=2023-04-04}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[Ji Yeon Kim|Kim Ji-yeon]] and Mandy Böhm took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.itnwwe.com/mma/ji-yeon-kim-vs-mandy-bohm-rebooked-ufc-may-13/|title=Ji Yeon Kim vs Mandy Böhm rebooked at UFC May 13 Fight Night|publisher=itnwwe.com|author=Vipin Raghuwanshi|date=2023-02-23|access-date=2023-03-03}} They were previously scheduled to meet at [[UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Spivak]], but Böhm was forced to withdraw due to illness.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/2/4/23586127/ufc-vegas-68-loses-mandy-bohm-vs-ji-yeon-kim-just-hours-before-the-event|title=UFC Vegas 68 loses Mandy Bohm vs. Ji Yeon Kim just hours before the event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2023-02-04|access-date=2023-02-04}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Jake Matthews (fighter)|Jake Matthews]] and [[Gabriel Green (fighter)|Gabriel Green]] was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/02/22/gabe-green-vs-jake-matthews-welterweight-match-added-may-13-ufc-event/|title=Gabe Green vs. Jake Matthews welterweight match-up added to May 13 UFC event|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2023-02-22|access-date=2023-02-22}} However, Matthews withdrew from the bout due to injury and was replaced by [[The Return of The Ultimate Fighter: Team Volkanovski vs. Team Ortega]] middleweight winner [[Bryan Battle]].{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/03/29/bryan-battle-vs-gabe-green-set-may-13-ufc-event/|title=Bryan Battle vs. Gabe Green set for May 13 UFC event|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2023-03-29|access-date=2023-03-29}} At the weigh-ins, Battle weighed in at 173 pounds, two pounds over the welterweight non-title fight limit. Their bout proceeded at catchweight and he was fined a percentage of his purse which went to Green.{{Cite since [[UFC on ESPN: Covington vs. Lawler]] in August 2019.\n\nA [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Aljamain Sterling]] and former [[UFC Flyweight Championship|UFC Flyweight]] and [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|Bantamweight Champion]] [[Henry Cejudo]] headlined the event.{{Cite Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.thescore.com/mma/news/2609567|title=Tsarukyan-Moicano set for April 29 UFC main headline the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.telecomasia.net/news/mma/tsarukyan-vs-moicano-fight-to-headline-ufc-fight-night-223-in-april/|title=Tsarukyan vs. Moicano fight to headline UFC Fight Night 223 in April|publisher=telecomasia.net|author=Marina Magomedova|date=2023-03-15|access-date=2023-03-15}} However, Moicano withdrew due to an injury two weeks before the event and the bout was canceled.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/04/ufc-fight-night-223-renato-moicano-injured-out-main-event-vs-arman-tsarukyan|title=Renato Moicano injured, out of UFC Fight Night 223 main event vs. Arman Tsarukyan|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday|author=Mike Bohn and Nolan King|date=2023-04-13|access-date=2023-04-13}} As a result, a bantamweight bout between [[Song Yadong]] and former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Bantamweight Championship|LFA Bantamweight Champion]] [[Ricky Simón]] was moved from [[UFC Fight Night: Pavlovich vs. Blaydes]] to instead headline this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/04/ufc-fight-night-223-news-ricky-simon-vs-song-yadong-moved-to-new-main-event|title=Ricky Simon vs. Song Yadong moved from UFC Fight Night 222, now took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mma.uno/cody-brundage-vs-rodolfo-vieira-reprogramado-para-ufc-fight-night-del-29-de-abril/|title=Cody Brundage vs. Rodolfo Vieira rescheduled for UFC against [[Journey Newson]]. [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on May 20, 2023, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/4/18/23688937/raquel-pennington-vs-irene-aldana-2-announced-for-ufc-vegas-73-main-event|title=Raquel Pennington vs. Irene Aldana 2 announced for UFC Vegas 73 main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2023-04-18|access-date=2023-04-18}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-news-five-las-vegas-events-confirmed-apex-spring-summer-2023|title=UFC officially books five more events in Las Vegas – four at UFC Apex|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2023-04-25|access-date=2023-04-25}}\n\n==Background==\nA women's bantamweight rematch between former [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Raquel Pennington]] and [[Irene Aldana]] was expected to headline the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/04/ufc-news-irene-aldana-vs-raquel-pennington-may-20-fight-card|title=Irene Aldana vs. Raquel Pennington main event among additions to UFC card May 20|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2023-04-18|access-date=2023-04-18}} The pair previously met at [[UFC on ESPN: dos Anjos vs. Edwards]] in 2019 which Pennington won by split decision.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2019/07/20/ufc-san-antonio-results-raquel-pennington-snaps-skid-win-irene-aldana/|title=UFC San Antonio results: Raquel Pennington snaps skid with split-decision win over Irene Aldana|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Heather Harshman|date=2019-07-19|access-date=2023-04-18}} They were also briefly expected to headline [[UFC on ESPN: Vera vs. Sandhagen]], but the bout never materialized.{{Cite web|author=Vipin Raghuwanshi|date=2023-01-26|title=Irene Aldana vs Raquel Pennington 2 to headline UFC San Antonio|url=https://www.itnwwe.com/mma/irene-aldana-vs-raquel-pennington-2-headline-ufc-san-antonio/|access-date=2023-01-27|publisher=itnwwe.com}} In turn, Aldana was pulled from the contest in early May in order to serve as a replacement in a [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] bout against current champion [[Amanda Nunes]] at [[UFC 289]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/05/ufc-289-news-irene-aldana-vs-amanda-nunes-julianna-pena-rib-injury|title=Irene Aldana replaces Julianna Peña at UFC 289, challenges Amanda Nunes for title|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2023-05-02|access-date=2023-05-02}} Pennington will serve as a backup for that event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/05/ufc-289-news-dana-white-raquel-pennington-backup-for-amanda-nunes-vs-irene-aldana-title-fight|title=Dana White: Raquel Pennington will be backup for Amanda Nunes vs. Irene Aldana title fight at UFC 289|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2023-05-05|access-date=2023-05-06}}\n\nAs a result, a women's strawweight bout between [[Mackenzie Dern]] and former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Strawweight Championship|Invicta FC Strawweight Champion]] [[Angela Hill]] was moved from [[UFC on ABC: Rozenstruik vs. Almeida]] one week prior to instead headline this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/05/ufc-fight-night-224-mackenzie-dern-vs-angela-hill-rescheduled-headliner|title=Mackenzie Dern vs. Angela Hill shifts from UFC on ABC 4 to UFC Fight Night 224 headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Samano|date=2023-05-08|access-date=2023-05-08}}\n\nA women's strawweight bout between former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Strawweight Championship|Invicta FC Strawweight Champion]] [[Emily Ducote]] and [[Polyana Viana]] was originally scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Song vs. Simón]].{{Cite web|url=https://agfight.com/ufc/ufc-encaminha-polyana-viana-vs-emily-ducote-para-card-de-abril/|title=UFC books Polyana Viana vs Emily Ducote to April card|publisher=agfight.com|author=Diego Ribas|date=2023-02-27|access-date=2023-03-06}} {{in lang|pt-br}} The pairing was eventually rescheduled for this event for undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-fight-night-223-fight-card-changes-ducote-viana-levy-rodriguez-quinlan-waters-loosa|title=UFC Fight Night 223 hit with three changes in days leading up to fight card|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2023-04-25|access-date=2023-04-25}} In turn, Viana withdrew from the bout and was replaced by former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Women's Strawweight Championship|LFA Women's Strawweight Champion]] [[Lupita Godinez]] at a catchweight of 120 pounds.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/05/08/loopy-godinez-replaces-polyana-viana-emily-ducote-ufc-vegas-73/|title=Loopy Godinez replaces Polyana Viana against Emily Ducote at UFC Vegas 73|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2023-05-08|access-date=2023-05-13}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Abdul Razak Alhassan]] and [[Brunno Ferreira]] was expected to take place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmarocks.pl/abdul-razak-alhassan/abdul-razak-alhassan-zmierzy-sie-z-bruno-ferreira-na-majowym-wydarzeniu-ufc|title=Abdul Razak Alhassan will face Bruno Ferreira at a UFC event in May|publisher=mmarocks.pl|author=Hubert Zdun|date=2023-02-23|access-date=2023-05-16}} {{in lang|pl}} However, the bout was moved to [[UFC on ESPN: Strickland vs. Magomedov]] for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportskeeda.com/amp/mma/news-middleweight-fight-moved-reportedly-added-ufc-fight-night-sean-strickland-vs-magomedov|title=Middleweight fight moved and reportedly added to UFC Fight Night: Strickland vs Magomedov|publisher=sportskeeda.com|author=Krishna Venki|date=2023-04-21|access-date=2023-05-16|archive-date=2023-05-16|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20230516190626/https://www.sportskeeda.com/amp/mma/news-middleweight-fight-moved-reportedly-added-ufc-fight-night-sean-strickland-vs-magomedov|url-status=dead}}\n\nA flyweight bout between Clayton Carpenter and [[Stephen Erceg]] was expected to take place at this event, but it was postponed due Erceg having visa issues.{{Cite web|url=https://thewest.com.au/sport/ufc/west-australian-stephen-ercegs-ufc-debut-against-clayton-carpenter-delayed-because-of-visa-troubles-c-10631952|title=West Australian Stephen Erceg's UFC debut against Clayton Carpenter delayed because of visa troubles|publisher=thewest.com.au|author=Mitchell Woodcock|date=2023-05-12|access-date=2023-05-19}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Vanessa Demopoulos]] and Orion Cosce missed weight. Demopoulos weighed in at 117.5 pounds and Cosce weighed in at 172.5 pounds, both one and half pounds over the women's strawweight and welterweight non-title fights limit respectively. Both bouts proceeded at catchweight with both being fined 20 percent of their purses, which went to their opponents: former [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] challenger (also former [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Women's strawweight championship|KSW Women's Flyweight Champion]]) [[Karolina Kowalkiewicz]] and Gilbert Urbina respectively.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/5/19/23729943/ufc-vegas-73-weigh-in-results-mackenzie-dern-angela-hill-hit-mark-for-headliner-two-fighters-miss|title=UFC Vegas 73 weigh-in results: Mackenzie Dern, Angela Hill hit the mark for headliner, two fighters miss|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2023-05-19|access-date=2023-05-19}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (ESPN+)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Strawweight\n|[[Mackenzie Dern]]\n|def.\n|[[Angela
